# Pātimokkha audit — rearranged for the TSV source

Source audit: written 2026-11-10 against the old `docs/` md files, then re-keyed to `data/patimokkha_word_by_word.tsv` (the old file is deleted). **No suggestion was added, changed or judged; only items still present in the TSV were kept.** For the issue #24 review. On 2026-11-13 the items the owner approved (corpus-wide section and rules NI to SA 11, line 1076) were applied to the TSV and removed from this list; the corpus-wide and rule items left here are the ones marked `(?)` / `(??)` and everything after SA 11 line 1076. Applying them did not shift any TSV line number.

## How to read

- `TSV n` is the physical line in `data/patimokkha_word_by_word.tsv` (quoted cells can span lines, so it is not the record number). Lines shift after edits: apply from the bottom up, or re-locate by rule code + `pali` cell.
- Sections follow TSV order, headed by rule code (column `abbrev`) and `source` name. Items spanning several rules are in 'Corpus-wide'. Tags: `grammar`, `gloss`, `translation`, `format`, `convention` (needs a decision first), `pali-text` (check edition), `doubtful` (not confirmed), `same-word`.
- Multi-row items show the first rows and a total.

## Dropped as already fixed or obsolete in the TSV

- Missing sentence translations (all but the last line `sādhu sādhu sādhu!` now have one); `#REF!` row; placeholder `… 4` headings; duplicated headings and row blocks; raw `<br>` (now line breaks inside cells, kept only where listed); grammar-cell typos (`mac`, `isntr`, `sadnhi` …); placeholders `?` / `“”` / `ta + ?`; stray `, comp` on `bhikkhu`, `bhikkhunā`, `dhammā`, `nissaggiyaṃ`; `dutiyo` / `chappañca` tags; `dhāretabbaṃ`; missing `of` / `sg` in `iminā`, `cīvaraṃ`, `paṭinissaggāya`, `adhikaraṇaṃ`, `niṭṭhitā`; `bhagavant`; `eḷakaloma:42`; `mahānāma` split; `bāhuppacālaka` construction; `saṅghabheda` `uddeso` gloss; `ayaṃ`, `eso`, `hi`, `tāva`, `uposatha`, `capucapu` inconsistencies; `ariṭṭha` `no` row; `jivhānicchāraka` row; sandhi/`matante`/`dulladdhaṃ` POS items; pass `of sīssa`-type items listed below only where still present.
- POS-gender rows for `apanidhāna`, `ujjhānasaññī`, `paṭhamasenāsana` (already `noun`).

### SA 11 · bhedānuvattaka (TSV from line 1008)

- TSV 1085 `[grammar]` `assu`: `pron | opt 3rd pl of assa` → POS verb, of atthi — **re-check: adjust: TSV 1085 POS `pron` → `verb`; keep grammar `opt 3rd pl of assa` (DPD assu 3.1 has "of assa", not "of atthi") (8)**
- TSV 1102 `[gloss]` `āyasmantānampi` "and for the venerables" → "for the venerables too" — **re-check: keep (8)**
- TSV 1108 `[grammar]` `ruccittha`: `aor 2nd pl of rucci`, base `(pass)` → aor 3rd sg (middle) of ruccati; subject is `saṅghabhedo` — **re-check: adjust: TSV 1108 grammar `aor 2nd pl of rucci` → `aor 3rd sg of ruccati` (subject saṅghabhedo; DPD rucci = aor of ruccati); base `√ruc + ya > rucca (pass)` → `√ruc + ya > rucca` (7)**
- TSV 1120 `[gloss]` "Patimokkha" spelling — **re-check: adjust: TSV 1120 meaning "following one Patimokkha" → "following one Pātimokkha" (as SA 10 line 960) (9)**
- TSV 1125 `[translation]` "Let the community be agreeable for the venerables" — with `saṅghena` the venerables are to agree with the community (`saṅghabheda` has it right); the rows differ too (`+dat` against `+instr`) — **re-check: adjust: TSV 1125 "Let the community be agreeable for the venerables, because" → "Let the venerables agree with the community, because" (matches 1111 gloss and SA 10 line 967) (7)**

### SA 12 · dubbaca (TSV from line 1156)

- TSV 1220 `[gloss]` `akāsi` "(venerable) make!" — stands with `mā`, prohibitive — **re-check: drop: the `mā` row glosses "don't!", so `akāsi` "(venerable) make!" already gives the prohibitive; the grammar `aor 3rd sg of karoti` matches DPD (6)**
- TSV 1259 `[grammar]` `vuṭṭḥāpana` → `vuṭṭhāpana` — **re-check: keep (10)**

### SA 13 · kuladūsaka (TSV from line 1292)

(8)**

- TSV 1398, 1442 `[grammar]` construction `bhaya + gāmino` → `bhaya + gāmī` (as the parallel rows) — **re-check: keep (8)**
- TSV 1414 `[translation]` "Because of this kind offence" — **re-check: adjust: TSV 1414 "Because of this kind offence" → "Because of such an offence" (8)**
- TSV 1426 `[gloss]` `avaca` "one says | one said" — stands with `mā`, prohibitive — **re-check: adjust: TSV 1426 meaning "one says" → "(venerable) speak!" (prohibitive with mā, same pattern as akāsi 1220); literal "one said" stays (7)**

Method: each of the seven slices of `data/patimokkha_word_by_word.tsv` was read line by line (Fable, Opus-reviewed), every Pāḷi item was checked against DPD, and every excerpt was checked with `verify_rows.py`. Rows the old audit already has are left out.
Date: 2026-11-14. Location is `patimokkha_word_by_word.tsv:<line>` against the TSV as edited on 2026-11-13.
Conf. below 8 means "check before changing". Rows with no concrete Suggested are in category H.
